18 10:44:29     consumption.  Is that correct?

20 10:44:37     correct order.  It's not really about power
21 10:44:42     consumption.

                                      95
 1 10:44:58     what's running on this server is our OpenBlue Bridge.

12 10:45:42     you know, sending itself over a particular threshold.

14 10:45:51     it's performing activities properly without being
